- **structural-variant-callset-matching**: Use when you have a VCF of predicted non-reference sequence insertions and need precision/recall against a PacBio validation callset, or a confirmation rate against a T2T-assembly-derived insertion benchmark. - **structural-variant-coordinate-reporting**: Use when long-read (PacBio/ONT) sequencing data is aligned to a reference genome and a large-scale structural rearrangement such as a tandem duplication or deletion needs breakpoint-resolved coordinate annotation. - **structural-variant-detection-from-short-read-alignments**: Use when you have mapped short-read BAM files for multiple strains or samples and need preliminary SV calls filtered to a high-specificity consensus set (e.g. &gt;=2-caller support). - **structural-variant-gold-set-assembly**: Use when a machine-learning SV caller needs labelled positive/negative training events, i.e. when real (non-simulated) gold-set call sets must be downloaded, interval-merged and converted into labelled feature vectors. - **structural-variant-pathogenicity-prediction**: Use when you need to classify non-coding or coding SVs (inversions, translocations, deletions, duplications in hg19) as pathogenic vs non-pathogenic for congenital-abnormality phenotypes and recover the causative gene and long-range pathomechanism. - **structural-variant-phasing-accuracy-benchmarking**: Use when long nanopore reads must be assigned to parental haplotypes via trio-phased heterozygous SNP anchors, or when SV calls need phasing from haplotype-assigned flanking reads and the result must be validated against trio-phased SNP ground truth.
